## Session 93 — List 1 (Dashboard, Sessions, Memory, Logs) — `dbSessionFields` + `parseAssistantLines` helpers + `MessageBubble` `fnName` reuse


Pick was List 1 (per session 92's "Next session should" recommendation — the longest gap in recent protocol history, last touched at session 89, and 3+ known follow-ups queued). Three byte-equivalent refactors targeted small but well-scoped duplications that survived the prior shallow sweep: a 4-field envelope shared across 2 route branches, a 4-line line-parser shared between mission-output and (transitively) the JSONL branch, and a 1-expression `String(fn?.name || "unknown")` repeated in a single component.

All **1290** unit tests pass (**197** suites, +13 new tests in `session-detail-helpers.test.ts` covering the 2 new helpers). tsc clean, eslint `--max-warnings 0` clean, build passes. Net: -19 deletions, +241 insertions (most of the insertions are the new test cases + the new helpers themselves, all of which will be exercised by future migrations). Byte-equivalent at runtime for all 5 affected call sites.

### Refactor 1 — `dbSessionFields(dbSession, sanitizedId)` in `src/lib/session-detail.ts`

`GET /api/sessions/[id]` has 2 branches that build a `SessionData` payload from a `SessionRecord` row — the mission-output-file branch (transcript exists at the mission output file) and the no-output-yet branch (mission-spawned session whose agent hasn't produced a file yet). Both branches shared the same 4 envelope fields derived from the row:

```ts
title: dbSession.title || sanitizedId,
model: dbSession.modelId || "",
source: dbSession.source,
created: dbSession.startedAt,
```

Repeated verbatim across 2 call sites, 4 lines each = 8 lines of identical-looking-but-could-drift envelope construction. Extracted to `dbSessionFields()` which returns the 4 fields as a plain object spread into the `buildSessionData({...})` call. Each call site collapses from 4 envelope lines to a single `...dbSessionFields(dbSession, sanitizedId)` spread. The 2 branches diverge in `format/filename/messages/size/missionId/note` and those stay inline.

**Byte-equivalence audit:** the helper returns `{title, model, source, created}` with the same `||` fallback discipline as the inline form. `title: dbSession.title || sanitizedId` falls back on null AND empty string (because `||` is truthy-falsy, not null-coalescing) — the helper preserves that. Verified by 6 unit tests including a byte-equivalence matrix that asserts the helper equals the inline form for 5 row shapes (full, null title, null modelId, empty-string title + modelId, null startedAt).

### Refactor 2 — `parseAssistantLines(content)` in `src/lib/session-detail.ts`

The mission-output-file branch read the file body and parsed it via:

```ts
const lines = content.split("\n").filter((l: string) => l.trim());
const messages = lines.map((line: string, i: number) => ({
  index: i,
  role: "assistant",
  content: line,
}));
```

The same split+filter+map skeleton appears in the JSONL branch (line 216) but with a different `.map()` body (`{index, ...msg}` after `JSON.parse(line)`), so the JSONL branch stays inline. The mission-output branch's body is simpler and worth lifting to a unit-testable parser — extracted to `parseAssistantLines(content)` which returns the same `Array<{index, role, content}>` shape.

**Why this isn't premature abstraction:** the parser is pure (no filesystem, no DB), has 3+ non-trivial behaviors to test (blank-line drop, filtered-index numbering, content verbatim preservation), and is the kind of utility a future export-to-JSONL tool or a `/api/sessions/[id]/export` endpoint would want. Verified by 7 unit tests including a byte-equivalence matrix against the inline form for 6 input shapes.

**Rejected:** extracting a `parseSessionLines(content, parser)` generic that would unify the mission-output and JSONL branches. The per-line parsing logic is different (mock assistant vs JSON.parse with try/catch fallback) and a generic would require either a callback parameter or a discriminated return type. The "Rule of Three" doesn't apply — only 2 consumers with different downstream shapes. The `parseAssistantLines` lift is a single-purpose helper for the 1 branch that uses it; promote to a generic on a 3rd consumer.

### Refactor 3 — `MessageBubble.tsx` `fnName` reuse

`MessageBubble.tsx` had `String(fn?.name || "unknown")` computed twice for the same `fn`:

```ts
const fnName = String(fn?.name || "unknown");   // line 169 (for the React key)
const tcKey = `toolcall-${i}-${fnName.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, "-")}`;
// ... 8 lines later:
<span className="text-neon-green">
  {String(fn?.name || "unknown")}                // line 177 (recomputed for the rendered label)
</span>
```

The second expression re-derives the same string for the rendered label. Reused the existing `fnName` local — the rendered text becomes `{fnName}` (the same `String(fn?.name || "unknown")` value, no recomputation, no behavior change).

**Why this is byte-equivalent:** `fnName` is a `const` in the same `.map((tc, i) => {...})` callback scope, holding `String(fn?.name || "unknown")` for the current `tc`. The JSX `{fnName}` interpolation calls `String()` on it implicitly, producing the same string the inline `{String(fn?.name || "unknown")}` produced. No `useMemo` or external state — purely a local constant reuse.

**Rejected:** promoting to a `<ToolCallName fn={fn} />` micro-component. The 2 sites are in the same `.map()` callback and the extraction would be 1 line of component definition for 1 line of JSX reuse — over-engineering for a same-scope variable reuse. The inline constant is the simplest form.

### What was rejected

- **Migrating `(main)/logs/page.tsx` to `useApiData({refreshIntervalMs})`** — session 91/89's follow-up. Would require extending the `useApiData` hook to absorb the manual `useInterval` + `setRefreshing` micro-state, which is a behavior change (removes the "Refresh" button's micro-spinner). The skill explicitly flags this as "AT LEAST identical results" → not a byte-equivalent move. Defer to a future session that opts in to the behavior change.
- **Migrating `(main)/sessions/page.tsx`'s `loadSessions` to `useApiData({queryParams})`** — would require extending the hook to support `URLSearchParams` and pagination. Same behavior-change concern. Defer until `useApiData` shape stabilizes across the logs + sessions + dashboards consumers.
- **Extracting `dbSessionFields` to a 2-arg `buildSessionDataForDb(dbSession, sanitizedId, overrides)` helper that builds the full payload** — would absorb the rest of the branch-specific fields (format/filename/messages/size/missionId/note) and unify the 2 branches into a single call. The shape of the 2 branches diverges enough (one returns size from `st.size`, the other from `dbSession.size`; one returns `missionId: dbSession.missionId`, the other `missionId: dbSession.missionId ?? null`; one has a `note`, the other doesn't; one has a file-derived `messages`, the other has `messages: []`) that the "unified" call would need 5+ override parameters. The 4-field spread keeps each branch's divergence explicit and readable. The 2-consumer Rule of Three doesn't justify the more abstract helper yet.
- **Migrating the inline `} catch (err) { showToast("Failed to X", "error") }` sites in `(main)/sessions/page.tsx:309` and `(main)/logs/page.tsx:81, 224`** to `toastError`. Both use **hardcoded strings** (not the caught error's message), so `toastError` would show the actual API error instead of the hardcoded string — a behavior change, NOT byte-equivalent. Leave inline.
- **Extracting `String(s)` coercion helpers from `MessageBubble.tsx:84, 119, 169, 177`** — only line 169/177 had the exact `String(fn?.name || "unknown")` form, so the lift was local. Lines 84 (`content || ""` for clipboard) and 119 (`String(msg.name)` for a name badge) are different expressions and stay inline.
- **The `useApiData` migration in `(main)/sessions/page.tsx`** for `loadSessions` — would need `URLSearchParams` + pagination support. Defer per the logs-page rationale.
- **The `({["all", "error", "warning"] as const}).map(...)` literal in `src/app/page.tsx:826`** for the errors panel severity filter. The `as const` + `.map()` is already the type-safe idiom; promoting to a `ERROR_SEVERITIES` constant in `@/lib/` would be 1 consumer (dashboard) and adds a file boundary for no testability gain. Defer until a 2nd consumer wants the same 3-value enum.

### Files

- `src/lib/session-detail.ts` (MODIFIED) — added `dbSessionFields` + `parseAssistantLines` helpers + `DbSessionEnvelope` type
- `src/app/api/sessions/[id]/route.ts` (MODIFIED) — 2 call sites migrated to `dbSessionFields`, 1 call site migrated to `parseAssistantLines`
- `src/components/session/MessageBubble.tsx` (MODIFIED) — 1 inline `String(fn?.name || "unknown")` replaced with the existing `fnName` const
- `tests/unit/session-detail-helpers.test.ts` (MODIFIED) — added 13 new tests (6 for `dbSessionFields`, 7 for `parseAssistantLines`)

Net diff: **4 files modified, +241 / -19 lines** (the +241 is dominated by the 13 new test cases + the 2 new helper definitions; the -19 is the 8-line envelope collapse × 2 + the 5-line line-parser collapse + the 2-line MessageBubble change).

### Verification

- **All 1290 unit tests pass** (197 suites, no regressions, +13 new tests covering the 2 new helpers and their byte-equivalence contract)
- **`npx tsc --noEmit`** clean (verified the `DbSessionEnvelope` structural type accepts a `SessionRecord` row via subtyping)
- **`CI=true npx eslint . --max-warnings 0`** clean
- **`npm run build`** passes
- **Byte-equivalence audit:** `dbSessionFields(row, id)` produces the same `{title: row.title || id, model: row.modelId || "", source: row.source, created: row.startedAt}` for every row shape (verified by the byte-equivalence matrix in `tests/unit/session-detail-helpers.test.ts`). `parseAssistantLines(content)` produces the same `Array<{index, role, content}>` as the inline form for every input shape (verified by the second byte-equivalence matrix). `MessageBubble.tsx`'s `{fnName}` reuses the same `String(fn?.name || "unknown")` result the inline form computed. Wire format, response shape, and rendered output are all unchanged.

### Patterns to take forward

1. **"Shared envelope helper for sibling route branches" pattern** — when 2 route branches share 3+ identical envelope fields and diverge on the rest, extract just the shared envelope to a `xxxFields(source, fallback)` helper. The 2 branches keep their divergence explicit. The alternative (a full `xxxResponse(source, override)` helper) absorbs all divergence into override parameters and obscures the branch-specific logic. The envelope-only helper is the lighter touch; promote to the full builder on a 3rd consumer.
2. **"Same-shape parser, different downstream consumer" anti-pattern** — when 2 sites do `content.split("\n").filter(...).map(...)` but the `.map()` body is different (mission-output uses a mock, JSONL uses `JSON.parse`), don't extract a generic `parseLines(content, callback)`. The callback parameter adds noise for 2 consumers with different downstream shapes. Extract only the sites that have IDENTICAL downstream bodies; leave the divergent sites inline.
3. **"Same-callback `{const}` reuse" pattern** — when a `.map((x, i) => {...})` callback computes the same value at the top and uses it in 2+ places inside the callback, just reuse the const. Don't promote to a sub-component, don't extract a helper. The variable reuse is the simplest form.

### "Next session should:" block

1. **Pick a different list next session.** List 1 has now been hit twice in a row (sessions 89, 93). The next pick should be List 2 (Cron, Missions, Chat) — last hit at session 88, the longest current gap after List 1's 2x streak. List 2 still has known follow-ups: `useMissionsPage` decomposition (1175+ LOC, the biggest hook in the codebase) and the 22-site `err instanceof Error ? err.message` sweep in `src/lib/cron/`, `src/lib/sync/`, `src/lib/backends/`.

Once `useApiData` supports query params, the same hook can absorb `(main)/logs/page.tsx`'s manual `useInterval` + `setRefreshing` pattern. Both migrations are behavior changes (the `setRefreshing` micro-state disappears), so they need an "AT LEAST identical results" opt-in. Recipe is in this session's "What was rejected" block.
3. **Promote `dbSessionFields` to a generic `extractEnvelopeFields<T, K>(source, fields, fallbacks)`** if a 2nd envelope-extraction appears (e.g. `extractAgentFields(agent)` in an agents route). The current 1-consumer Rule of Three doesn't justify a generic; defer until 2 distinct envelope shapes want the same treatment.
4. **Promote `parseAssistantLines` to a generic `parseLines<T>(content, parseLine)`** if a 2nd consumer wants the "split + filter blank + map per line" shape with a different per-line parser. The current 1-consumer Rule of Three doesn't justify the generic; defer until a 2nd branch or a future export endpoint needs it.
5. **The 4 `} catch { showToast("Failed to X", "error") }` sites in `src/app/operations/`** are the next refactor target when a future session explicitly opts in to the "AT LEAST identical results = strictly more informative" interpretation. Recipe is in session 92's "What was rejected" block.
6. **Sweep the 22 inline `err instanceof Error ? err.message` sites in `src/lib/sync/`, `src/lib/cron/`, `src/lib/backends/`, `src/lib/hermes-profile-sync.ts` (7 sites alone) and the `src/components/cron/` operations pages** to a server-side `toMessage(e, fallback)` helper. Multi-session sweep, deferred to a List 2/3/4 session with the budget to handle 8+ files.

8. **HindsightBrowser 4 inline `error ?? "..."` sites** — would benefit from a `runMutation`-shaped variant. Defer until a 2nd consumer wants the same shape.

## Session 92 — List 4 (Models, HERMES.md, Environment, All Settings) — `pushDiff` closure refactor in 2 routes

Pick was List 4 (per random selection `python3 -c "import random; random.seed(20260603); print(random.choice([1,2,3,4]))"` = 4, the first List-4 pick since session 90). Session 90's analysis had concluded List 4 was "mined clean" — this session targeted two short, well-scoped closure extractions that escaped the previous sweep's shallow-cosmetic scope: 9 inline `diffs.push({id, label, detail})` sites in `/api/models/[id]/diff/route.ts` and 4 inline `diffs.push({field, before, after}) + updates.X = Y` site pairs in `/api/models/sync/pull/route.ts`'s `computeDiffs` helper. Both extractions are pure byte-equivalent closures that collapse repeated `diffs.push({...})` boilerplate into a single token per call.

All **1277** unit tests pass (**197** suites, no new tests added — both routes are already covered by `tests/unit/models-import-api.test.ts` + `tests/unit/models-pull-context-length.test.ts`). tsc clean, eslint `--max-warnings 0` clean, build passes. Net: -18 lines (44 insertions, 62 deletions). Byte-equivalent at runtime for all 13 affected call sites.

### Refactor 1 — `pushDiff(id, label, detail)` closure in `src/app/api/models/[id]/diff/route.ts`

The route's POST handler had 9 inline `diffs.push({id, label, detail})` calls spread across the push + pull branches (5 in the push branch: modelId/provider/baseUrl/credential/no-change; 4 in the pull branch: no-hermes-data/modelId/provider/baseUrl/no-change). The shape is identical — a 3-field object appended to a `DiffEntry[]` array. Extracted a local closure:

```ts
const pushDiff = (id: string, label: string, detail: string) => {
  diffs.push({ id, label, detail });
};
```

Each of the 9 sites collapsed from a 4-line object literal to a 1-line `pushDiff(id, label, detail)` call. The closure captures the `diffs` array via lexical scope, so no extra parameter is needed. The wire shape (`{ data: { diffs, modelName } }`) is identical — the closure is a 1:1 transformation of the 9 object-literal sites.

### Refactor 2 — `pushDiff(field, before, after)` closure in `src/app/api/models/sync/pull/route.ts`

`computeDiffs()` had 4 inline `diffs.push({field, before, after}) + updates.X = Y` site pairs (modelId, provider, baseUrl, contextLength). Each site had to (1) push a diff record to the `diffs[]` accumulator and (2) record the new value on the `updates` object. Extracted a local generic closure with a type-safe `field` parameter:

```ts
const pushDiff = <K extends keyof typeof hermes & keyof typeof model>(
  field: K,
  before: unknown,
  after: unknown,
) => {
  diffs.push({ field: field as string, before, after });
  updates[field] = after;
};
```

Each of the 4 sites collapsed from 2-4 lines (the `diffs.push({...})` + `updates.X = Y` pair) to a 1-line `pushDiff(field, before, after)` call. The generic constraint `keyof typeof hermes & keyof typeof model` correctly limits `field` to the 4 shared keys (`modelId`, `provider`, `baseUrl`, `contextLength`) — TypeScript's structural typing rejects any other field name at the call site. The `field as string` cast is required because TypeScript can't narrow through a generic index. The wire shape (the `{diffs, updates}` return object) is identical.

### What was rejected

- **Extracting `pushDiff` to a shared `@/lib/diff-helpers.ts` module.** Only 2 consumers (the diff route and the pull route), and the closures have different signatures (3 strings vs 3 generics). Sharing would require a single over-parameterised signature or a thin wrapper module — neither is a clear win over 2 file-local closures. Promote to a shared lib on 3rd consumer.
- **Using a class field or a `diffs.reduce()` chain** instead of a closure. A closure is the most idiomatic JS pattern for "operate on the enclosing array" — a `reduce` would require re-binding the array, and a class adds OOP overhead for a 4-line function. The closure is the simplest form.
- **Migrating the 4 `diffs.push({id, label, detail})` sites in the `SyncModal` `fetchDiffs` fallback in `src/components/models/ModelSyncButtons.tsx`.** Component-side, not route-side. Different shape (`{id, label, detail}` with a `provider/modelIdString`-derived fallback, not a `diffs[].field` accumulator). The component's fallback array is built inline from a hardcoded set of diff labels, not from a diff-accumulation loop. Not the same pattern — the closure refactor doesn't apply. Out of scope.
- **Migrating the inline `for (const task of Object.keys(aux)) maskApiKeyField(...)` loop in `src/app/api/config/route.ts:97-100` to `Object.values(aux).forEach(...)`.** Session 90 already evaluated this and called it "marginal leverage (1 LOC saved, no testability gain)". Left alone per session 90's guidance.
- **The 4 `} catch { showToast("Failed to X", "error") }` sites in `src/app/operations/`** (carryover from session 90). Migrating to `toastError` is NOT byte-equivalent (it would show the actual API error message instead of the hardcoded string). Per session 91's "scope expansion" precedent, this is a future-session target when a session explicitly opts in to the "strictly more informative" interpretation of the constraint. The recipe is in `references/control-hub-list3-refactor-session-92.md`.

### Files

- `src/app/api/models/[id]/diff/route.ts` (MODIFIED) — `pushDiff` closure added, 9 sites collapsed
- `src/app/api/models/sync/pull/route.ts` (MODIFIED) — `pushDiff` closure added inside `computeDiffs`, 4 site pairs collapsed

Net diff: **2 files modified, +44 / -62 lines** (the +44 includes the 2 closures + their JSDoc-style inline comments; the -62 is the 13 collapsed site bodies).

### Verification

- **All 1277 unit tests pass** (197 suites, no regressions, no new tests — both routes are already exhaustively covered by `tests/unit/models-import-api.test.ts` and `tests/unit/models-pull-context-length.test.ts`)
- **`npx tsc --noEmit`** clean (verified the generic `keyof typeof hermes & keyof typeof model` constraint compiles)
- **`CI=true npx eslint . --max-warnings 0`** clean
- **`npm run build`** passes
- **Byte-equivalence audit:** both closures push the same object literal to the same array (or set the same key on the same object). The wire shape (`{ data: { diffs, ... } }` in the diff route and `{ data: { diffs, ... } }` + the `updates` arg to `updateModel()` in the pull route) is unchanged. The diff is a name-and-indirection refactor, not a behavior change.

### Patterns to take forward

1. **"Local closure over accumulator" pattern** — when 3+ inline `diffs.push({...})` calls in the same function share the same shape, extract a `pushX(...args)` closure that captures the accumulator. The closure pattern is more idiomatic than `array.reduce()` for "operate on the enclosing array" because it doesn't re-bind the array on every call. 2 file-local closures in this session; promote to `@/lib/` on 3rd consumer.
2. **"Generic `pushDiff<K extends keyof T>(field: K, before, after)" pattern** — when the closure needs a type-safe key parameter, the `keyof typeof obj` constraint is the right tool. It rejects invalid key names at the call site AND captures the type for the `updates[field] = after` write. The `as string` cast on the push site is required because TypeScript can't narrow a generic index through a `diffs.push` call; this is a known TS limitation, not a bug.
3. **"Closure vs shared lib" discriminator** — 2 file-local closures is fine. 3+ would justify a `@/lib/diff-helpers.ts` (or wherever the consumer module lives). Don't over-engineer at 2 consumers.

### "Next session should:" block

1. **Pick a different list next session.** List 4 has now been hit 5+ times (sessions 60, 70, 73, 85, 86, 90, 92). **List 1 is the next-ripe surface** — hasn't been touched since session 89 (the longest gap in recent protocol history). Carries 3 known follow-up items from session 89: `(main)/logs/page.tsx` `useApiData` migration, `(main)/sessions/page.tsx` URLSearchParams + pagination extension, and the inline `err instanceof Error` site in `JobFormModal.tsx` for the setError migration.
2. **Promote `pushDiff` closure to `@/lib/diff-helpers.ts`** if a 3rd consumer appears. The current 2 consumers (diff route + pull route) don't share enough shape to justify a shared module — the diff route uses `{id, label, detail}` (3 strings), the pull route uses `{field, before, after}` (generic key + 2 unknowns). A shared module would need both signatures. Defer until a 3rd route or lib function wants one of them.
3. **The 4 `} catch { showToast("Failed to X", "error") }` sites in `src/app/operations/`** are the next refactor target when a future session explicitly opts in to the "AT LEAST identical results = strictly more informative" interpretation. Recipe is in `references/control-hub-list3-refactor-session-92.md`.
4. **Migrate `(main)/logs/page.tsx` to `useApiData({ refreshIntervalMs })`** — extends the hook to absorb the manual `useInterval` + `setRefreshing` pattern. The extension is a behavior change (removes the micro-state), so document the trade-off in a follow-up PR.
5. **`useApiData` extension for `URLSearchParams` + pagination** — `(main)/sessions/page.tsx`'s `loadSessions` is the next candidate. Requires extending the hook to support query params. Defer until the hook shape is stable across logs + sessions.
6. **`useMissionsPage` decomposition** — 1175+ LOC, still the biggest hook in the codebase. List 2 territory. Out of scope for "AT LEAST identical results" — would need a careful hook-by-hook extraction with state-derivation verification.

The 7 `src/lib/hermes-profile-sync.ts` sites alone are enough to justify the helper. Multi-session sweep.

## Session 91 — List 3 (Models, Agents, Skills, Tools, Personalities) — `setErrorFromCaught` helper + 9-site migration

Pick was List 3 (per random selection `echo $(( RANDOM % 4 + 1 ))` = 3, second List-3 pick in a row). This session picks up the explicit carryover from session 90's "Next session should:" block: promote `setErrorFromCaught` to `@/lib/api-fetch.ts` and migrate the 7 (now 9) `setError(messageFromError(err, "..."))` sites that the audit had been tracking. Single byte-equivalent refactor.

All **1277** unit tests pass (**197** suites — +1 new suite `set-error-from-caught.test.ts` with 8 new tests). tsc clean, eslint `--max-warnings 0` clean, build passes. Byte-equivalent at runtime for all 9 migrated sites.

### Refactor 1 — `setErrorFromCaught` helper promotion

Added `setErrorFromCaught(setError, err, fallback)` to `src/lib/api-fetch.ts` as the `setError` sibling of the existing `toastError(showToast, err, fallback)` helper. Same composition rules (toError() + `|| fallback` discipline), different consumer. The new helper is the canonical replacement for the 9 sites that did `setError(messageFromError(err, "..."))` — one fewer intermediate variable and one less line in the common catch block.

Byte-equivalence to the inline form is documented in the JSDoc and proven by the byte-equivalence test in `tests/unit/set-error-from-caught.test.ts`:
```ts
directSetError(messageFromError(err, fallback));
setErrorFromCaught(helperSetError, err, fallback);
expect(helperCalls).toEqual(directCalls);
```
Both code paths produce the same `setError` call for 6 different caught-value shapes (Error, empty Error, string, null, undefined, TypeError).

### Refactor 2 — 9-site migration

| # | File | Line | Migration | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `src/app/operations/personalities/page.tsx` | 166 | `setErrorFromCaught(setError, err, "Unknown error")` | List 3 — dropped `messageFromError` from import (was the only use) |
| 2 | `src/app/operations/skills/[...path]/page.tsx` | 66 | `setErrorFromCaught(setError, err, "Unknown error")` | List 3 — dropped `messageFromError` from import (was the only use) |
| 3 | `src/hooks/useModelsPage.ts` | 97 | `setErrorFromCaught(setError, err, "Failed to load registry")` | List 3 — kept `messageFromError` import (still used for `details[].detail` and bulk-update `error: msg`) |
| 4 | `src/components/models/ModelEditor.tsx` | 161 | `setErrorFromCaught(setError, err, "Save failed")` | List 3 — dropped `messageFromError` from import (was the only use) |

4 of the 9 sites are in List 3 territory (the focus of this session's pick), 4 are in List 4 (config pages), 1 is in List 1 (sessions). The carryover is fully resolved: session 90 noted 7 sites with the inline form. This session found 2 more (the verbose `setError(toError(err).message || "...")` form in `config/[section]/page.tsx`, which is byte-equivalent to the same `setError(messageFromError(err, "..."))` form), bringing the total to 9 and closing the loop.

### What was rejected

- **Migrating the `setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error")` site in `src/components/cron/JobFormModal.tsx:166` and the 4 sites in `src/app/recroom/story-weaver/create/page.tsx` + `src/app/api/stories/route.ts`.** These are in Lists 1/2/3 and 4 (recroom/stories is its own category), not in this session's List 3 focus. They're all in scope for a future `setErrorFromCaught` extension, but pulling them in here would expand the session's scope beyond the List 3 pick. **9 sites across 7 files is plenty for a single session** — the 22 inline `err instanceof Error ? err.message` sites across the codebase are the next-tier refactor surface and will need a dedicated multi-session sweep.
- **Migrating `setGenError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error")` in `src/app/recroom/story-weaver/create/page.tsx`.** Same as above — recroom/stories territory, out of scope for a List 3 pick.
- **Migrating the 22 `err instanceof Error ? err.message : <fallback>` sites in `src/lib/sync/`, `src/lib/cron/`, `src/lib/backends/`, etc. to a server-side `toMessage(e, fallback)` helper.** The `messageFromError(e, fallback)` is already a server-safe helper (it only depends on `toError` which is pure JS). A dedicated sweep would touch `lib/sync/SyncScheduler.ts`, `lib/sync-manager.ts`, `lib/backends/hermes.ts`, `lib/cron/hermes-sync.ts`, `lib/hermes-profile-sync.ts` (7 inline sites in just that one file). Out of scope — would be a multi-session lib sweep, not a List 3 pick.
- **Extending the `setErrorFromCaught` signature to accept the React Dispatch type directly via a `React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string | null>>` import.** Adds React coupling to `api-fetch.ts` (which is currently a pure-TS module shared with server code). The current `SetErrorFn` local alias preserves the public type without coupling.
- **Promoting `setErrorFromCaught` to accept a 4th `prevState: T | null` parameter for `setError((prev) => ...)` updater pattern.** None of the 9 migrated sites use the updater form. Rule of Three says: don't add the parameter until you need it.

### Verification

- **All 1277 unit tests pass** (197 suites, +1 suite with +8 new tests in `tests/unit/set-error-from-caught.test.ts`)
- **`npx tsc --noEmit`** clean
- **`CI=true npx eslint . --max-warnings 0`** clean
- **`npm run build`** passes
- **Byte-equivalence audit per session-51 lesson:** `setErrorFromCaught(setError, err, fallback)` is the canonical implementation of `setError(messageFromError(err, fallback))` (`src/lib/api-fetch.ts:151`). The migration is a name change, not a behavior change. The verbose `setError(toError(err).message || fallback)` form in `config/[section]/page.tsx` is byte-equivalent to `setError(messageFromError(err, fallback))` because `toError(err).message || fallback` is literally the body of `messageFromError`. So the 2 verbose sites get the same byte-equivalence treatment.

### Patterns to take forward

1. **"setErrorFromCaught is the setError sibling of toastError" pattern** — two parallel helpers in `api-fetch.ts`, one per consumer. The audit recipe is a 1-line grep: `rg "setError\(messageFromError" src/` (after this session: 0 hits) or `rg "setError\(toError" src/` (after this session: 0 hits). New code review should reject both inline forms.
2. **"Verbose `toError(err).message || fallback` is the same as `messageFromError(err, fallback)`"** — they have identical semantics. The helper form is preferred because it has a name and JSDoc, but the verbose form is not a bug. Sites that use the verbose form can either keep it (with a JSDoc comment explaining the equivalence) or migrate to `setErrorFromCaught`. This session chose to migrate.
3. **"Import `messageFromError` if and only if you have a non-setError, non-showToast consumer"** — 5 of the 9 migrated files dropped `messageFromError` from their imports entirely. Only `useModelsPage.ts` kept it (for `details[].detail` and bulk-update return). `setErrorFromCaught` is a strict subset of `messageFromError`'s consumer surface.

### "Next session should:" block

1. **Pick a different list next session** to spread the refactor surface. List 3 has been hit 7+ times now (sessions 67, 70, 77, 80, 90, 91). **List 1 is the next-ripe surface** — hasn't been touched since session 87 (the longest gap in recent protocol history). Carries 3 known follow-up items: `(main)/logs/page.tsx` `useApiData` migration, `(main)/sessions/page.tsx` URLSearchParams + pagination extension, and the inline `err instanceof Error` site in `JobFormModal.tsx` for the setError migration.
2. **Sweep the 22 inline `err instanceof Error ? err.message` sites in `src/lib/`, `src/components/cron/`, and `src/app/recroom/`** to a server-side `toMessage(e, fallback)` or a `setErrorFromCaught`-shaped helper. The 7 `src/lib/hermes-profile-sync.ts` sites alone are enough to justify the helper. Multi-session sweep.
3. **`useApiData` extension for `URLSearchParams` + pagination** — `(main)/sessions/page.tsx`'s `loadSessions` is the next candidate. Requires extending the hook to support query params. Defer until the hook shape is stable across logs + sessions.
4. **Migrate `(main)/logs/page.tsx` to `useApiData({ refreshIntervalMs })`** — extends the hook to absorb the manual `useInterval` + `setRefreshing` pattern. The extension is a behavior change (removes the micro-state), so document the trade-off in a follow-up PR.
5. **`useMissionsPage` decomposition** — 1175+ LOC, still the biggest hook in the codebase. List 2 territory. Out of scope for "AT LEAST identical results" — would need a careful hook-by-hook extraction with state-derivation verification.
6. **Status badge label override follow-up** — `StatusBadge.tsx:106` has a pre-existing bug where the fallback label for unknown mission statuses uses `titleCase(unknown_status)` instead of the canonical "Queued". Documented in session 89's "rejected" block. A future session can fix it.
7. **HindsightBrowser 4 inline `error ?? "..."` sites** — would benefit from a `runMutation`-shaped variant. Defer until a 2nd consumer wants the same shape.

## Session 90 — List 3 (Models, Agents, Skills, Tools, Personalities) — 4-site `toastError` migration in operations pages

Pick was List 3 (per random selection `echo $(( RANDOM % 4 + 1 ))` = 3, the first List-3 pick since session 80). Single byte-equivalent refactor: a 4-site migration of the inline `showToast(messageFromError(err, "..."), "error")` pattern in `src/app/operations/` to the `toastError(showToast, err, "...")` helper that was promoted in sessions 77 + 79. The helper was already adopted in `src/hooks/useModelsPage.ts` (9 sites) but the operations pages had been left untouched. This session closes the last 4 sites in List 3 territory.

All 1269 unit tests pass (196 suites, no new tests added — the helper is exhaustively tested in `tests/unit/toast-error.test.ts`). tsc clean, eslint `--max-warnings 0` clean, build passes. Byte-equivalent at runtime for all 4 migrated sites.

### Refactor 1 — 4 `showToast(messageFromError(...))` → `toastError(...)` migrations

| File | Line | Migration |
|---|---|---|
| `src/app/operations/skills/page.tsx` | 168 | `toastError(showToast, err, "Failed to update skill")` — toggleSkill catch block |
| `src/app/operations/skills/page.tsx` | 216 | `toastError(showToast, err, "Failed to save skill")` — saveSkillEdit catch block |
| `src/app/operations/tools/page.tsx`  | 72  | `toastError(showToast, err, "Failed to load toolsets")` — loadToolsets catch block |
| `src/app/operations/agents/page.tsx` | 207 | `toastError(showToast, e, "Failed to load file")` — openFile catch block (uses `e` not `err`) |

All 4 sites are clean catch-block patterns with no extra side effects on the error message (the skills toggle site has a `setData(prevData)` revert BEFORE the toast, which is preserved unchanged). The migration is mechanical and byte-equivalent: `toastError(showToast, err, fallback)` produces the same `showToast(messageFromError(err, fallback), "error")` call as the inline form (per `src/lib/api-fetch.ts:128`).

### Why this matters

The `toastError` helper is the canonical "catch-block error unwrap → toast" pattern. With this session, **every catch-block toast in `src/app/operations/` now uses the helper**, matching the convention in `useModelsPage.ts`. The `showToast(messageFromError(...))` form is now an outlier — it survives in `(main)/logs/page.tsx`, `config/seed/page.tsx`, and a few other List 1/4 sites, but is fully eliminated from List 3.

### Audit recipe (to find any future inline site)

```bash
grep -rnE "showToast\(messageFromError\(" src/ --include="*.ts" --include="*.tsx" \
  | grep -v "api-fetch\|tests/"
```

**Pre-session: 4 hits in List 3** (the 4 migrated sites). **Post-session: 0 hits in List 3** + 0 hits in `src/lib/api-fetch.ts` (those are JSDoc examples, not real call sites).

### What was NOT migrated (and why)

- `src/app/operations/skills/[...path]/page.tsx:66` — `setError(messageFromError(err, "Unknown error"))`. Uses `setError` (a useState setter), not `showToast`. The `toastError` helper is for `showToast`-based error reporting. A `setErrorFromCaught(setError, err, fallback)` helper would be the equivalent, but with only 7 sites total across the codebase and no 2nd-consumer pattern, the Rule of Three says to keep the inline form.
- `src/app/operations/personalities/page.tsx:166` — same as above (`setError` not `showToast`).
- `src/hooks/useModelsPage.ts:97,175,253` — same: `setError` (line 97) and `details[].detail` (line 175, 253). Not toast candidates.

### Files

- `src/app/operations/skills/page.tsx` (MODIFIED) — 2 sites migrated, import changed `messageFromError` → `toastError`
- `src/app/operations/tools/page.tsx` (MODIFIED) — 1 site migrated, import changed
- `src/app/operations/agents/page.tsx` (MODIFIED) — 1 site migrated, import changed

Net diff: **3 files, +7 / -7 lines** (each migration is a 1-line replacement + a 1-character import change).

### Verification

- **All 1269 unit tests pass** (196 suites, no new tests — `toastError` is already exhaustively covered by `tests/unit/toast-error.test.ts`)
- **`npx tsc --noEmit`** clean
- **`CI=true npx eslint . --max-warnings 0`** clean on all 3 modified files
- **`npm run build`** passes
- **Byte-equivalence audit per session-51 lesson:** `toastError(showToast, err, fallback)` is the canonical implementation of `showToast(messageFromError(err, fallback), "error")` (`src/lib/api-fetch.ts:128`). The migration is a name change, not a behavior change.

### What was rejected

- **Migrating the 7 `setError(messageFromError(err, "..."))` sites to a `setErrorFromCaught` helper.** With only 7 sites across 6 files, the Rule of Three says to keep the inline form. The helper would be `setErrorFromCaught(setError, err, fallback)` — a 3-parameter setter wrapper. Not a clear win.
- **Refactoring `apiFetch` to accept `body?: unknown` like `safeApiCall` does, eliminating the 12 `JSON.stringify({...})` wrappers in List 3 callsites.** This is a signature-level change with type-level ripples across the entire codebase. The current convention (`apiFetch` takes `RequestInit`, `safeApiCall` takes a body object) is documented in the JSDoc and is consistent across all 30+ callsites. Out of scope.
- **Consolidating the 4 `Modal` import + 4 `useToast` + 4 `toastElement` patterns in the 4 operations pages into a shared `useOperationPage` hook.** Out of scope — the 4 pages have different state shapes, different modals, and different load/save semantics. A shared hook would have 4+ conditional branches and wouldn't reduce code.
- **Extracting the `{ showToast, toastElement } = useToast()` + `{toastElement}` render pattern into a custom `useToastElement()` hook that returns the element directly.** Cosmetic only, doesn't reduce code at the call site. Defer.
- **Auditing the inline `console.warn("Model auto-import failed", msg)` in `useModelsPage.ts:67` for a `logApiError` migration.** Different surface (server-side logging, not toasts). Already uses the canonical `toError(err).message || String(err)` pattern from session 79. No change needed.

### Patterns to take forward

1. **"List 3 operations pages are now `toastError`-complete" pattern** — the 4 sites that remained after the session-77 + session-79 migrations are all closed. The `showToast(messageFromError(...))` inline form is now an anti-pattern in operations pages, and code review should reject it. The audit recipe is a 1-line grep.
2. **"Different catch-block consumer" discriminator for `toastError` vs `setError`/`details[].detail`** — `toastError` is only for `showToast`-based reporting. When a catch block writes to `setError` (a useState setter) or to a structured `details[].detail` field, the `messageFromError(err, fallback)` form is correct (it returns the message; the caller decides where to put it). The 7 sites in `src/app/operations/skills/[...path]/page.tsx`, `personalities/page.tsx`, `useModelsPage.ts` are all of this shape.

### "Next session should:" block

1. **Pick a different list next session** to spread the refactor surface. List 3 has been hit 6+ times now (sessions 67, 70, 77, 80, 90). **List 4 is the next-ripe surface** — it hasn't been touched since session 86 (the longest gap in the protocol history), and has accumulated 2 carry-over items from session 86: `safeWriteFileAtomic` helper promotion and `safeReadJson` helper promotion.
2. **`setErrorFromCaught` helper promotion** — when the 8th `setError(messageFromError(err, ...))` site appears (currently 7), promote to a `setErrorFromCaught(setError, err, fallback)` helper. The 7th site is most likely to appear in List 4 territory.
3. **Migrate `(main)/logs/page.tsx` to `useApiData({ refreshIntervalMs })`** — extends the hook to absorb the manual `useInterval` + `setRefreshing` pattern. The extension is a behavior change (removes the micro-state), so document the trade-off in a follow-up PR.
4. **`useApiData` extension for `URLSearchParams` + pagination** — `(main)/sessions/page.tsx`'s `loadSessions` is the next candidate. Requires extending the hook to support query params. Defer until the hook shape is stable across logs + sessions.
5. **`useMissionsPage` decomposition** — 1175+ LOC, still the biggest hook in the codebase. List 2 territory. Out of scope for "AT LEAST identical results" — would need a careful hook-by-hook extraction with state-derivation verification.
6. **`Sidebar.tsx`'s `openCheckDropdown` / `handleDropdownConfirm` / `doCheck` triplet** — 3 similar patterns for the version-check dropdown. Lower-value than the deploy consolidation done in session 78, but a candidate.

## Session 132 — List 3 (Models, Agents, Skills, Tools, Personalities) — `ok()` factory migration of 3 missed sites + filter-scope-mismatch fix

Pick was **List 3** via `echo $((RANDOM % 4 + 1))` = 3, the first List-3 pick since session 130. Random pick: **List 3**. Session 130 documented a known gap (session 130 P-130-1, "filter-scope-mismatch") — the per-list page set in the mission brief is page-named (Models/Agents/Skills/Tools/Personalities = 5 pages) and maps to 6 backend subtrees, but the `ok-factory-source-patterns.test.ts` List 3 filter only covered 3 of the 6 subtrees (`api/models`, `api/agent`, `api/credentials`). The 3 missing subtrees (`api/skills`, `api/tools`, `api/personalities`) silently let 3 un-migrated `data:` sites through. This session closes the gap.

### 1. Filter extension (3 subtrees added)

The List 3 production it() block's filter was extended from:

```ts
s.file.includes(`${join("src", "app", "api", "models")}`) ||
s.file.includes(`${join("src", "app", "api", "agent")}`) ||
s.file.includes(`${join("src", "app", "api", "credentials")}`),
```

to:

```ts
s.file.includes(`${join("src", "app", "api", "models")}`) ||
s.file.includes(`${join("src", "app", "api", "agent")}`) ||
s.file.includes(`${join("src", "app", "api", "credentials")}`) ||
s.file.includes(`${join("src", "app", "api", "skills")}`) ||
s.file.includes(`${join("src", "app", "api", "tools")}`) ||
s.file.includes(`${join("src", "app", "api", "personalities")}`),
```

Per session 130 P-130-3: the page-named List 3 → backend-subtree mapping is **6 subtrees**, not 3. The block comment was updated to document the mapping for future maintainers.

### 2. 3 un-migrated `data:` sites discovered + migrated

Running the test BEFORE the migration (with the extended filter) catches the 3 sites:

```
Found 3 un-migrated 'return NextResponse.json({ data: ... })' site(s) in the List 3 surface:
  src/app/api/personalities/route.ts:51  return NextResponse.json({
  src/app/api/personalities/route.ts:78  return NextResponse.json({
  src/app/api/tools/route.ts:19  return NextResponse.json({
```

All 3 sites are 200-locked success responses, byte-equivalent to `ok(...)`:

| File | Line | Site | Migration |
|------|------|------|-----------|
| `src/app/api/personalities/route.ts` | 51 | `return NextResponse.json({ data: { success: true, name, prompt, source: "SOUL.md" } })` (PUT/POST success) | `ok({ success: true, name: result.profile, prompt, source: "SOUL.md" })` |
| `src/app/api/personalities/route.ts` | 78 | `return NextResponse.json({ data: { personalities, total } })` (GET success) | `ok({ personalities: profiles, total: profiles.length })` |
| `src/app/api/tools/route.ts` | 19 | `return NextResponse.json({ data: { platforms: HERMES_PLATFORMS, toolsets: HERMES_CONFIGURABLE_TOOLSETS } })` (GET toolset catalog) | `ok({ platforms: HERMES_PLATFORMS, toolsets: HERMES_CONFIGURABLE_TOOLSETS })` |

**Per-file commit granularity** (session 116 P-2): 2 separate commits, one per file. The `api/personalities` and `api/tools` routes are independent of each other, so a per-file commit makes the carryover-detection state clean if the session hits the iteration cap mid-file.

**Import cleanup** (session 130 P-130-4): `api/tools/route.ts` no longer uses `NextResponse` after the migration, so the import was dropped (the only reference was the migrated site). `api/personalities/route.ts` still uses `NextResponse` at line 17 for the `instanceof NextResponse` bodyResult check, so the import is retained.

**Per the pitfall from session 111 P-1** ("local-var shadowing when adding a new helper import"), I checked for any pre-existing `const ok = ...` in the modified files. Neither file had one, so no rename was needed. Per session 130 P-130-4, the post-migration `rg -c "NextResponse\." <file>` audit confirmed `api/tools/route.ts` had 0 references and `api/personalities/route.ts` had 1 (the `instanceof` check).

### 3. Filter-scope self-test (`scanner form coverage` block)

A new self-test was added to the `scanner form coverage` describe block to pin the filter-scope contract. The test reads this test file's source and asserts each of the 6 expected subtrees has a matching `join("src", "app", "api", "<name>")` call in the production filter.

**Why the design**: an earlier iteration of this self-test (path-based: synthesize fixture paths and run the production filter against them) was a false positive — it passed even with the `api/tools` clause removed, because both `expectedSubtrees.length` and `matched.length` shrunk by 1 in lockstep. The substring approach (read the test file's source, check for `join("src", "app", "api", "<name>")` literally) is robust because `expectedSubtrees` is a hard-coded constant — if a future session removes a clause from the production filter, `expectedSubtrees` stays at 6 and the substring check fails.

**Self-test verification**: temporarily removing the `api/tools` clause from the production filter makes the test fail with a clear diagnostic:

```
List 3 filter-scope self-test failed: the following expected subtrees are NOT
covered by the production filter: src/app/api/tools. Add the missing subtrees
to the List 3 filter in the 'has zero ... List 3 surface' it() block. Expected
subtrees (from mission brief + session 130 P-130-3): src/app/api/models,
src/app/api/agent, src/app/api/credentials, src/app/api/skills,
src/app/api/tools, src/app/api/personalities
```

The test was then restored and re-verified green.

### 4. What was NOT migrated (and why)

- **The 3 `ok()` sites in `api/skills/*` and the 18 in `api/models/*`** — already migrated in sessions 111-117. Re-verified via the `ok-factory-source-patterns.test.ts` source-pattern test (List 3 filter now covers all 6 subtrees, 0 un-migrated sites).
- **The `messageFromError` sites in `src/app/operations/`, `src/components/models/`, `src/hooks/useModelsPage.ts`** — already migrated in sessions 77 + 91. Re-verified by the audit recipe `rg -nU 'setError\(messageFromError' src/ | rg -v 'api-fetch|tests/'` (0 hits).
- **The `toastError` sites in operations pages** — already migrated in session 90. Re-verified by the audit recipe `rg -nE "showToast\(messageFromError\(" src/` (0 hits in `src/app/operations/`).
- **The `serverErrorFromCatch` sites in `api/skills/*` + `api/tools/*`** — already migrated in session 127. Re-verified by the `server-error-from-catch-source-patterns.test.ts` (List 3 surface assertion, 0 un-migrated sites).
- **The `apiFetch + JSON.stringify` sites in `useModelsPage.ts`** (e.g. line 170: `apiFetch(\`/api/models/sync/${action}\`, { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({...}) })`) — candidate for the session 131 P-131-2 `apiFetch → safeApiCall` migration, but the migration changes the field accessor (`d.data?.field` → `result.data?.data?.field`) and is a 1-file, 1-site change. Defer to a future List 3 session that explicitly opts in to accessor-shift migrations.
- **The `safeApiCall<{ data: T }>` envelope-unwrap bug-class sites in `useModelsPage.ts:413`** — same as above: candidate for the `safeApiCallData` helper migration, but the call is in List 3 territory and was deliberately not part of this session's scope (this session is about the source-pattern test, not the safeApiCallData helper).

### 5. Files

- `src/app/api/personalities/route.ts` (MODIFIED) — 1 import extension (`ok` added) + 2 site migrations + 1 `NextResponse` import retained (used at line 17)
- `src/app/api/tools/route.ts` (MODIFIED) — 1 import extension (`ok` added) + 1 site migration + 1 `NextResponse` import removed (per session 130 P-130-4)

Net diff: **2 source files + 1 test file, +112 / -17 lines**

### 6. Verification

- **1654 unit tests pass** (235 suites, +1 new self-test in `ok-factory-source-patterns.test.ts`)
- **`npx tsc --noEmit`** clean
- **`CI=true npx eslint . --max-warnings 0`** clean
- **`npm run build`** passes
- **Byte-equivalence audit per session 51 lesson**: `ok({...})` is literally `NextResponse.json({ data: {...} }, { status: 200 })` (per `src/lib/api-response.ts:197`). All 3 migrated sites are 200-locked success responses with `{ data: <T> }` envelopes — byte-equivalent.

### 7. Patterns to take forward

1. **"Filter-scope-mismatch" pitfall (session 130 P-130-1)** — the per-list page set in the mission brief is page-named; the source-pattern test filter is backend-subtree-named. They do not auto-sync. Cross-check at session start with the audit recipe: list all page-named members for the picked list, then `rg -nP` the filter paths in the source-pattern test. If a page-named member has no matching filter path, the scanner is silently blind to that subtree. The session-132 self-test in `ok-factory-source-patterns.test.ts` codifies this check as an automated regression test.
2. **"Self-test design: substring vs path-based" lesson** — for filter-coverage self-tests, substring checks against the production filter's literal source are more robust than path-based fixture injection. The path-based approach I tried first was a false positive — both `expected.length` and `matched.length` shrunk in lockstep when a clause was removed. Substring checks against a hard-coded `expectedSubtrees` array catch the failure mode correctly.
3. **"Per-file commit granularity" pattern (session 116 P-2 recall)** — when 2 files both need `ok()` migrations in the same session, commit them separately. The 3 commits this session produced (personalities → tools → test) are each self-consistent: imports updated, no dead imports, `npx tsc --noEmit` clean. The iteration cap can hit between commits without leaving any file in an inconsistent state.

### 8. "Next session should:" block

1. **Pick a different list next session** to spread the refactor surface. List 3 has been hit 10+ times now (sessions 67, 70, 77, 80, 90, 91, 112, 117, 119, 130, 131, 132). **List 1 is the next-ripe surface** — last touched session 129 (3 sessions ago), and the session-129 follow-ups are: (a) the `JobFormModal.tsx:166` inline `err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error"` site (was deferred from session 91 because the `setErrorFromCaught` helper had only 7 sites — the count is now 9 after session 91, so it's ripe for a 2-consumer → N-consumer promotion), (b) the `useApiData` extension for `URLSearchParams` + pagination in `(main)/sessions/page.tsx` (deferred from sessions 98, 109, 122, 123), and (c) the inline `console.error` redundancy rule from session 131 P-131-4 applied to `(main)/logs/page.tsx` and `(main)/sessions/page.tsx` (3-5 sites). **List 2** is also still ripe — the `api/cron/hardware/route.ts` 4-site dynamic-message migration from session 128 P-2 is still the next byte-equivalent surface. **List 4** is the quietest surface (last touched session 124) but most-ripe for new content discovery (the per-list source-pattern tests are CLOSED for all 4 lists now, so the next List 4 pick needs to find new refactor opportunities OUTSIDE the 4 factory families).
2. **`setErrorFromCaught` follow-up** — the 7-site count from session 91 is now 9 (the 2 added in `config/[section]/page.tsx` and `config/seed/page.tsx` are migrated in session 91). The 8th site is most likely to appear in List 1 territory (the `JobFormModal.tsx:166` site is the 8th candidate). If a future List 1 pick migrates that site, the `setErrorFromCaught` helper is justified (8+ consumers) and can be promoted to `api-fetch.ts` per session 91's pattern.
3. **Cross-list project status** — the `ok()` factory migration is now CLOSED for all 4 lists (verified via the per-list source-pattern tests, all 0 un-migrated sites). The next cross-list project is the dynamic-message `serverError` family (session 128 P-1, session 131 P-3) — 5 sites in `api/cron/hardware/route.ts` + 2 sites in `api/orchestration/chat` and 1 in `api/memory/hindsight/route.ts` = 8 dynamic-message sites that the `serverErrorFromCatch` source-pattern scanner intentionally does NOT match. A sister-helper `serverErrorFromError(route, context, error, prefix?)` would close all 8 sites in 1 helper + 1 sister-test. Multi-session sweep.
4. **`useApiData` extension for `URLSearchParams` + pagination** — `(main)/sessions/page.tsx`'s `loadSessions` is the next candidate. Requires extending the hook to support query params. Defer until the hook shape is stable across logs + sessions.
5. **`useMissionsPage` decomposition** — 1298+ LOC, still the biggest hook in the codebase. List 2 territory. Out of scope for "AT LEAST identical results" — would need a careful hook-by-hook extraction with state-derivation verification.
6. **Migrate the inline `apiFetch + JSON.stringify` sites in `useModelsPage.ts`** (e.g. line 170) to the `safeApiCall` helper per session 131 P-131-2 (the accessor shift `d.data?.field` → `result.data?.data?.field` is a byte-equivalent migration with the per-call accessor rewrite). 1-file, 1-2 site change. Defer to a future List 3 session that explicitly opts in to accessor-shift migrations.
7. **Promote `successMessageForDispatch` to `src/lib/`** when a 2nd consumer appears. Currently 1 consumer (useMissionsPage). List 2 territory.
